    // Crisp EdgesGeometry technical CAD linework (publication-grade in iso_paper)
    const edgeLines: THREE.LineSegments[] = [];
    if (isIsoPaper || isBlueprint || isToolpath || renderMode === 'gdt') {
      edgeLines.push(...applyTechnicalEdges(partGroup, mats.techLine, isIsoPaper ? 18 : 24));
    }

    // Center partGroup cleanly around (0,0,0) while preserving child anchorObj positions
    _boxTmp.setFromObject(partGroup);
    _boxTmp.getCenter(_centerTmp);
    _boxTmp.getSize(_sizeTmp);
    partGroup.position.sub(_centerTmp);
    const boundingRadius = Math.max(2.6, _sizeTmp.length() * 0.5);

    // Sync React metadata catalogs for Quick Sub-Part Rail & Dimension Bars
    setSubPartCatalog(subPartEntries.map((e) => e.spec));
    setDimensionBarsMeta(dimBarEntries.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, label: d.label })));

    // Explicit 3D GD&T Datum Planes (A/B/C) when renderMode === 'gdt'
    const gdtGroup = new THREE.Group();
    gdtGroup.userData._excludeExport = true;
    if (renderMode === 'gdt') {
      const planeSize = boundingRadius * 2.05;
      const makeDatumPlane = (color: number, rotX: number, rotY: number, offset: THREE.Vector3) => {
        const pMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          color,
          transparent: true,
          opacity: 0.16,
          side: THREE.DoubleSide,
          depthWrite: false
        });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(planeSize, planeSize), pMat);
        mesh.rotation.set(rotX, rotY, 0);
        mesh.position.copy(offset);
        const border = new THREE.LineSegments(
          new THREE.EdgesGeometry(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(planeSize, planeSize)),
          new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color })
        );
        mesh.add(border);
        return mesh;
      };

      gdtGroup.add(makeDatumPlane(0x38bdf8, Math.PI / 2, 0, new THREE.Vector3(0, -_sizeTmp.y * 0.52, 0)));
      gdtGroup.add(makeDatumPlane(0xf97316, 0, 0, new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -_sizeTmp.z * 0.52)));
      gdtGroup.add(makeDatumPlane(0x10b981, 0, Math.PI / 2, new THREE.Vector3(-_sizeTmp.x * 0.52, 0, 0)));
    }

    // Live CNC Helical Contour Toolpath + Animated Carbide Endmill Spindle when renderMode === 'toolpath'
    const toolpathGroup = new THREE.Group();
    toolpathGroup.userData._excludeExport = true;
    let camCurve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3 | null = null;
    let spindleAssembly: THREE.Group | null = null;
    if (isToolpath) {
      const pathPts: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
      const rx = Math.max(1.4, _sizeTmp.x * 0.56);
      const ry = Math.max(1.2, _sizeTmp.y * 0.54);
      const rz = Math.max(1.4, _sizeTmp.z * 0.56);
      const passes = Math.max(8, Math.min(20, Math.round(260 / Math.max(8, camStepoverPct))));
      const totalSamples = passes * 24;
      for (let i = 0; i <= totalSamples; i++) {
        const u = i / totalSamples;
        const theta = u * passes * Math.PI * 2;
        const yPass = ry - u * (ry * 1.9);
        const radiusWave = 1.0 + 0.08 * Math.sin(theta * 4);
        pathPts.push(
          new THREE.Vector3(
            Math.cos(theta) * rx * radiusWave,
            yPass,
            Math.sin(theta) * rz * radiusWave
          )
        );
      }
      camCurve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pathPts, true);
      const lineGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(camCurve.getPoints(360));
      const lineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
        color: 0x22d3ee,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.78
      });
      toolpathGroup.add(new THREE.Line(lineGeo, lineMat));

      // Stock bounding wireframe cage
      const stockBox = new THREE.LineSegments(
        new THREE.EdgesGeometry(new THREE.BoxGeometry(_sizeTmp.x * 1.12, _sizeTmp.y * 1.12, _sizeTmp.z * 1.12)),
        new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf97316, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45 })
      );
      toolpathGroup.add(stockBox);

      // Carbide Ball-Nose Endmill + HSK-63A Collet Chuck
      spindleAssembly = new THREE.Group();
      const chuck = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.32, 1.1, 20),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x94a3b8, metalness: 0.9, roughness: 0.22 })
      );
      chuck.position.y = 1.35;
      spindleAssembly.add(chuck);

      const flute = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14, 0.14, 1.35, 16),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
          color: 0xfbbf24,
          emissive: 0xd97706,
          emissiveIntensity: 0.45,
          metalness: 0.95,
          roughness: 0.15
        })
      );
      flute.position.y = 0.68;
      spindleAssembly.add(flute);

      const tip = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.14, 14, 14),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x22d3ee })
      );
      tip.position.y = 0;
      spindleAssembly.add(tip);

      toolpathGroup.add(spindleAssembly);
    }

    const assemblyRoot = new THREE.Group();
    assemblyRoot.add(partGroup);
    assemblyRoot.add(gdtGroup);
    assemblyRoot.add(toolpathGroup);
    scene.add(assemblyRoot);
    partGroupRef.current = partGroup;

    // Cameras
    const aspect = width / height;
    const perspCam = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(34, aspect, 0.1, 200);
    const orthoHalf = boundingRadius * 1.48;
    const orthoCam = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(
      -orthoHalf * aspect,
      orthoHalf * aspect,
      orthoHalf,
      -orthoHalf,
      -100,
      200
    );

    const activeCam: THREE.Camera = projection === 'orthographic' ? orthoCam : perspCam;
    const initDist = boundingRadius * 2.75;
    perspCam.position.set(initDist * 0.74, initDist * 0.52, initDist * 0.80);
    orthoCam.position.copy(perspCam.position);
    perspCam.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
    orthoCam.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    const quadTopCam = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-orthoHalf, orthoHalf, orthoHalf, -orthoHalf, -100, 200);
    quadTopCam.position.set(0, initDist, 0.01);
    quadTopCam.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    const quadFrontCam = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-orthoHalf, orthoHalf, orthoHalf, -orthoHalf, -100, 200);
    quadFrontCam.position.set(0, 0, initDist);
    quadFrontCam.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    const quadRightCam = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-orthoHalf, orthoHalf, orthoHalf, -orthoHalf, -100, 200);
    quadRightCam.position.set(initDist, 0, 0);
    quadRightCam.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    const controls = new OrbitControls(activeCam, renderer.domElement);
    controls.enableDamping = !isSoftwareGl;
    controls.dampingFactor = 0.07;
    controls.minDistance = 2.0;
    controls.maxDistance = 48.0;
    controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);

    // Click Raycaster Selection (distinguishing click vs orbit drag)
    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    let downX = 0;
    let downY = 0;
    const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      downX = e.clientX;
      downY = e.clientY;
    };
    const onPointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (viewportLayoutRef.current !== 'single') return;
      const dist = Math.hypot(e.clientX - downX, e.clientY - downY);
      if (dist > 6) return;

      const rect = renderer!.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
      _rayNdc.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      _rayNdc.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
      raycaster.setFromCamera(_rayNdc, activeCam);

      const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(partGroup.children, true);
      let pickedId: string | null = null;
      for (let i = 0; i < hits.length; i++) {
        let cur: THREE.Object3D | null = hits[i].object;
        while (cur && cur !== partGroup) {
          if (cur.userData?.subPartId) {
            pickedId = cur.userData.subPartId as string;
            break;
          }
          if (cur.userData?.subPart?.id) {
            pickedId = cur.userData.subPart.id as string;
            break;
          }
          cur = cur.parent;
        }
        if (pickedId) break;
      }

      if (pickedId) {
        setSelectedSubPartId((prev) => (prev === pickedId ? null : pickedId));
      } else {
        setSelectedSubPartId(null);
      }
    };
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);

    captureFnRef.current = () => {
      renderer!.render(scene, activeCam);
      const url = renderer!.domElement.toDataURL('image/png');
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `${entry.drawingNumber}-${renderMode}.png`;
      link.click();
    };

    const onResize = () => {
      if (!container) return;
      const w = container.clientWidth || 1;
      const h = container.clientHeight || 1;
      const asp = w / h;
      perspCam.aspect = asp;
      perspCam.updateProjectionMatrix();
      orthoCam.left = -orthoHalf * asp;
      orthoCam.right = orthoHalf * asp;
      orthoCam.top = orthoHalf;
      orthoCam.bottom = -orthoHalf;
      orthoCam.updateProjectionMatrix();
      renderer!.setSize(w, h);
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);

    let frameId = 0;
    let disposed = false;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    function afterDraw(cb: () => void) {
      if (disposed) return;
      const gl = renderer!.getContext() as WebGL2RenderingContext | null;
      if (!gl || typeof gl.fenceSync !== 'function') {
        frameId = window.setTimeout(cb, 80);
        return;
      }
      const sync = gl.fenceSync(gl.SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE, 0);
      if (!sync) {
        frameId = window.setTimeout(cb, 80);
        return;
      }
      gl.flush();
      const poll = () => {
        if (disposed) {
          gl.deleteSync(sync);
          return;
        }
        const st = gl.getSyncParameter(sync, gl.SYNC_STATUS);
        if (st === gl.UNSIGNALED) {
          frameId = window.setTimeout(poll, 5);
        } else {
          gl.deleteSync(sync);
          frameId = window.setTimeout(cb, 80);
        }
      };
      frameId = window.setTimeout(poll, 5);
    }

    const tick = (now: number) => {
      if (disposed) return;
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;

      if (autoRotateRef.current && !camGoalRef.current.active && viewportLayoutRef.current === 'single') {
        assemblyRoot.rotation.y += dt * 0.24;
      }

      if (camCurve && spindleAssembly && camRunningRef.current) {
        const camT = (now * 0.000045) % 1;
        camCurve.getPointAt(camT, _projVec);
        spindleAssembly.position.copy(_projVec);
        spindleAssembly.rotation.y += dt * 18.0;
      }

      if (camGoalRef.current.active) {
        _camGoalPos.copy(camGoalRef.current.pos).normalize().multiplyScalar(initDist);
        activeCam.position.x = THREE.MathUtils.damp(activeCam.position.x, _camGoalPos.x, 8, dt);
        activeCam.position.y = THREE.MathUtils.damp(activeCam.position.y, _camGoalPos.y, 8, dt);
        activeCam.position.z = THREE.MathUtils.damp(activeCam.position.z, _camGoalPos.z, 8, dt);
        controls.target.lerp(_camTarget, 0.2);
        if (activeCam.position.distanceTo(_camGoalPos) < 0.06) {
          activeCam.position.copy(_camGoalPos);
          controls.target.copy(_camTarget);
          camGoalRef.current.active = false;
        }
      }

      const ax = sectionAxisRef.current;
      const pct = sectionPercentRef.current;
      if (ax === 'none') {
        clipPlane.normal.set(-1, 0, 0);
        clipPlane.constant = 100;
      } else if (ax === 'x') {
        clipPlane.normal.set(-1, 0, 0);
        clipPlane.constant = pct * (_sizeTmp.x * 0.55);
      } else if (ax === 'y') {
        clipPlane.normal.set(0, -1, 0);
        clipPlane.constant = pct * (_sizeTmp.y * 0.55);
      } else {
        clipPlane.normal.set(0, 0, -1);
        clipPlane.constant = pct * (_sizeTmp.z * 0.55);
      }

      controls.update();
      assemblyRoot.updateMatrixWorld(true);

      const w = container.clientWidth || width;
      const h = container.clientHeight || height;

      try {
        if (viewportLayoutRef.current === 'quad') {
          renderer!.setScissorTest(true);
          const halfW = Math.floor(w / 2);
          const halfH = Math.floor(h / 2);
          const qAsp = halfW / Math.max(1, halfH);

          const renderQuad = (cam: THREE.Camera, vx: number, vy: number) => {
            if (cam instanceof THREE.OrthographicCamera) {
              cam.left = -orthoHalf * qAsp;
              cam.right = orthoHalf * qAsp;
              cam.top = orthoHalf;
              cam.bottom = -orthoHalf;
              cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
            } else if (cam instanceof THREE.PerspectiveCamera) {
              cam.aspect = qAsp;
              cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
            }
            renderer!.setViewport(vx, vy, halfW, halfH);
            renderer!.setScissor(vx, vy, halfW, halfH);
            renderer!.render(scene, cam);
          };

          renderQuad(activeCam, 0, halfH);
          renderQuad(quadTopCam, halfW, halfH);
          renderQuad(quadFrontCam, 0, 0);
          renderQuad(quadRightCam, halfW, 0);
          renderer!.setScissorTest(false);
        } else {
          renderer!.setScissorTest(false);
          renderer!.setViewport(0, 0, w, h);
          renderer!.render(scene, activeCam);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        disposed = true;
        setWebglError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Component WebGL render failed.');
        return;
      }

      // 60fps Orthogonal Elbow Leader & ASME Dimension Projection (Zero React state churn)
      if (viewportLayoutRef.current === 'single' && showCalloutsRef.current) {
        const currentSelected = selectedSubPartIdRef.current;
        const leftColumn: Array<{ id: string; ax: number; ay: number }> = [];
        const rightColumn: Array<{ id: string; ax: number; ay: number }> = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < subPartEntries.length; i++) {
          const item = subPartEntries[i];
          item.anchorObj.getWorldPosition(_projVec);
          _projVec.project(activeCam);
          if (_projVec.z > 1) {
            const gEl = leaderGroupRefs.current[item.spec.id];
            if (gEl) gEl.style.opacity = '0';
            continue;
          }
          const px = (_projVec.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * w;
          const py = (-_projVec.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * h;

          const preferRight =
            item.spec.sideHint === 'right'
              ? true
              : item.spec.sideHint === 'left'
              ? false
              : px >= w * 0.48;

          if (preferRight) {
            rightColumn.push({ id: item.spec.id, ax: px, ay: py });
          } else {
            leftColumn.push({ id: item.spec.id, ax: px, ay: py });
          }
        }

        const placeColumn = (
          items: Array<{ id: string; ax: number; ay: number }>,
          isRight: boolean
        ) => {
          items.sort((a, b) => a.ay - b.ay);
          const minGap = items.length > 9 ? 19 : 23;
          const topBound = isIsoPaper ? 44 : 28;
          const botBound = h - 38;
          let prevY = topBound - minGap;

          const resolvedY: number[] = items.map((it) => {
            const targetY = Math.max(it.ay, prevY + minGap);
            const clamped = Math.min(botBound, targetY);
            prevY = clamped;
            return clamped;
          });

          // Bottom-up pass if pushed past bottom boundary
          for (let i = resolvedY.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (resolvedY[i + 1] - resolvedY[i] < minGap) {
              resolvedY[i] = Math.max(topBound, resolvedY[i + 1] - minGap);
            }
          }

          const shelfX = isRight ? w - 168 : 168;
          const kneeBaseX = isRight ? w - 208 : 208;

          for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
            const it = items[i];
            const ly = resolvedY[i];
            const gEl = leaderGroupRefs.current[it.id];
            const dotEl = leaderDotRefs.current[it.id];
            const pathEl = leaderPathRefs.current[it.id];
            const textEl = leaderTextRefs.current[it.id];
            if (!gEl || !dotEl || !pathEl || !textEl) continue;

            const isSel = currentSelected === it.id;
            const strokeColor = isSel
              ? '#ea580c'
              : isIsoPaper
              ? '#1e293b'
              : '#94a3b8';
            const textColor = isSel
              ? '#ea580c'
              : isIsoPaper
              ? '#0f172a'
              : '#f1f5f9';

            gEl.style.opacity = currentSelected && !isSel ? '0.62' : '1';
            dotEl.setAttribute('cx', it.ax.toFixed(1));
            dotEl.setAttribute('cy', it.ay.toFixed(1));
            dotEl.setAttribute('r', isSel ? '4.5' : '3');
            dotEl.setAttribute('fill', strokeColor);

            const kneeX = isRight ? Math.max(it.ax + 16, kneeBaseX) : Math.min(it.ax - 16, kneeBaseX);
            pathEl.setAttribute(
              'd',
              `M ${it.ax.toFixed(1)} ${it.ay.toFixed(1)} L ${kneeX.toFixed(1)} ${ly.toFixed(1)} L ${shelfX.toFixed(1)} ${ly.toFixed(1)}`
            );
            pathEl.setAttribute('stroke', strokeColor);
            pathEl.setAttribute('stroke-width', isSel ? '1.8' : '1.15');

            const tx = isRight ? shelfX + 6 : shelfX - 6;
            textEl.setAttribute('x', tx.toFixed(1));
            textEl.setAttribute('y', (ly + 3.5).toFixed(1));
            textEl.setAttribute('text-anchor', isRight ? 'start' : 'end');
            textEl.setAttribute('fill', textColor);
            textEl.setAttribute('font-weight', isSel ? '800' : '600');
          }
        };

        placeColumn(leftColumn, false);
        placeColumn(rightColumn, true);

        // Project ASME Double-Arrow Dimension Bars
        for (let d = 0; d < dimBarEntries.length; d++) {
          const dim = dimBarEntries[d];
          const gEl = dimGroupRefs.current[dim.id];
          const ext1 = dimExt1Refs.current[dim.id];
          const ext2 = dimExt2Refs.current[dim.id];
          const bar = dimBarRefs.current[dim.id];
          const bgRect = dimLabelBgRefs.current[dim.id];
          const txt = dimTextRefs.current[dim.id];
          if (!gEl || !ext1 || !ext2 || !bar || !bgRect || !txt) continue;

          dim.startObj.getWorldPosition(_projVec);
          _projVec.project(activeCam);
          dim.endObj.getWorldPosition(_projEnd);
          _projEnd.project(activeCam);

          if (_projVec.z > 1 || _projEnd.z > 1) {
            gEl.style.opacity = '0';
            continue;
          }

          const x1 = (_projVec.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * w;
          const y1 = (-_projVec.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * h;
          const x2 = (_projEnd.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * w;
          const y2 = (-_projEnd.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * h;

          const bx1 = x1 + dim.offsetX;
          const by1 = y1 + dim.offsetY;
          const bx2 = x2 + dim.offsetX;
          const by2 = y2 + dim.offsetY;

          gEl.style.opacity = '1';
          ext1.setAttribute('x1', x1.toFixed(1));
          ext1.setAttribute('y1', y1.toFixed(1));
          ext1.setAttribute('x2', bx1.toFixed(1));
          ext1.setAttribute('y2', by1.toFixed(1));

          ext2.setAttribute('x1', x2.toFixed(1));
          ext2.setAttribute('y1', y2.toFixed(1));
          ext2.setAttribute('x2', bx2.toFixed(1));
          ext2.setAttribute('y2', by2.toFixed(1));

          bar.setAttribute('x1', bx1.toFixed(1));
          bar.setAttribute('y1', by1.toFixed(1));
          bar.setAttribute('x2', bx2.toFixed(1));
          bar.setAttribute('y2', by2.toFixed(1));

          const mx = (bx1 + bx2) * 0.5;
          const my = (by1 + by2) * 0.5;
          const estW = Math.max(76, dim.label.length * 6.0 + 12);
          bgRect.setAttribute('x', (mx - estW * 0.5).toFixed(1));
          bgRect.setAttribute('y', (my - 8).toFixed(1));
          bgRect.setAttribute('width', estW.toFixed(1));
          bgRect.setAttribute('height', '16');

          txt.setAttribute('x', mx.toFixed(1));
          txt.setAttribute('y', (my + 3.5).toFixed(1));
        }
      }

      if (isSoftwareGl) {
        afterDraw(() => tick(performance.now()));
      } else {
        frameId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
    };

    if (isSoftwareGl) {
      tick(performance.now());
    } else {
      frameId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    const onContextLost = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      disposed = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
      clearTimeout(frameId);
      setWebglError('3D WebGL context was interrupted.');
    };
    const onContextRestored = () => {
      setWebglError(null);
      setRetryNonce((n) => n + 1);
    };
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onContextLost);
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onContextRestored);

    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      partGroupRef.current = null;
      highlightMatRef.current = null;
      captureFnRef.current = null;
      cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
      clearTimeout(frameId);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      renderer?.domElement.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      renderer?.domElement.removeEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
      renderer?.domElement.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onContextLost);
      renderer?.domElement.removeEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onContextRestored);
      controls.dispose();
      scene.traverse((obj) => {
        if (obj instanceof THREE.Mesh || obj instanceof THREE.Line || obj instanceof THREE.LineSegments) {
          obj.geometry?.dispose();
          if (Array.isArray(obj.material)) obj.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());
          else obj.material?.dispose();
        }
      });
      Object.values(mats).forEach((m) => m.dispose());
      edgeLines.forEach((l) => l.geometry.dispose());
      textures.dispose();
      if (scene.environment) scene.environment.dispose();
      renderer?.renderLists.dispose();
    };
  }, [
    partKey,
    config.bore,
    config.stroke,
    config.rodLength,
    config.deckHeight,
    config.chamberCc,
    config.pistonReliefCc,
    config.pistonAlloy,
    config.ringApplication,
    config.camProfile,
    config.rockerRatio,
    config.carbCfm,
    config.carbStyle,
    config.carbVenturiIn,
    config.airFilterCfmBonus,
    config.intakePortCc,
    config.intakeValveIn,
    config.exhaustPortCc,
    config.exhaustPortShape,
    config.exhaustValveIn,
    config.headerPrimaryIn,
    config.cylinders,
    config.ignitionTiming,
    config.sparkPlugGap,
    config.targetAFR,
    config.firingOrder,
    config.bellhousingPattern,
    specs.displacementCi,
    specs.quenchDistance,
    specs.ptwClearance,
    specs.rodStrokeRatio,
    specs.maxRodAngleDeg,
    specs.staticCR,
    specs.topRingGap,
    specs.secondRingGap,
    specs.totalValveLift,
    specs.intakeFlowPeakCfm,
    specs.exhaustFlowPeakCfm,
    specs.eiFlowRatioPct,
    specs.intakeVelocityFps,
    specs.effectiveCarbCfm,
    specs.peakCfmDemand,
    specs.carbSizingStatus,
    specs.camData.lobeLift,
    specs.camData.intakeDur050,
    specs.camData.exhDur050,
    specs.intakeData.name,
    specs.intakeData.style,
    specs.intakeData.flowCfm,
    renderMode,
    projection,
    retryNonce,
    entry.drawingNumber,
    camStepoverPct
  ]);

  const handleExportSTL = () => {
    if (!partGroupRef.current) return;
    const tris = exportObject3DToSTL(
      partGroupRef.current,
      `${entry.drawingNumber}-${partKey}.stl`
    );
    setExportStatus(`Exported ${tris.toLocaleString()} STL triangles (${entry.drawingNumber}.stl)`);
    setTimeout(() => setExportStatus(null), 4000);
  };

  const handleExportDXF = () => {
    exportPartProfileToDXF({
      drawingNumber: entry.drawingNumber,
      title: entry.partName,
      partKey,
      bore: config.bore,
      stroke: config.stroke,
      rodLength: config.rodLength,
      deckHeight: config.deckHeight,
      chamberCc: config.chamberCc,
      filename: `${entry.drawingNumber}-${partKey}.dxf`
    });
    setExportStatus(`Exported AutoCAD R12 DXF (${entry.drawingNumber}.dxf)`);
    setTimeout(() => setExportStatus(null), 4000);
  };

  const isIsoPaper = renderMode === 'iso_paper';

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
      {/* Top Individual Part CAD Toolbar */}
      <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0d1014] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Render / Engineering Mode */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-0.5 bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 p-0.5 rounded-md">
            {(
              [
                { id: 'cinematic', label: 'Cinematic PBR' },
                { id: 'iso_paper', label: 'Tech Paper ISO' },
                { id: 'pbr', label: 'Studio PBR' },
                { id: 'gdt', label: 'GD&T Datums' },
                { id: 'stress', label: 'FEA Stress' },
                { id: 'thermal', label: 'Thermal' },
                { id: 'toolpath', label: 'CNC Toolpath' },
                { id: 'blueprint', label: 'Blueprint' }
              ] as Array<{ id: ComponentRenderMode; label: string }>
            ).map((m) => (
              <button
                key={m.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onChangeRenderMode(m.id)}
                className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                  renderMode === m.id
                    ? 'bg-orange-600 text-white font-semibold'
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                }`}
              >
                {m.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Projection Mode */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 p-0.5 rounded-md">
            {(
              [
                { id: 'perspective', label: 'Persp' },
                { id: 'orthographic', label: 'Ortho CAD' }
              ] as Array<{ id: ComponentProjectionMode; label: string }>
            ).map((p) => (
              <button
                key={p.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onChangeProjection(p.id)}
                className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                  projection === p.id
                    ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                }`}
              >
                {p.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Single vs 4-Viewport Quad Ortho */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              onChangeViewportLayout(viewportLayout === 'single' ? 'quad' : 'single')
            }
            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1.5 transition whitespace-nowrap ${
              viewportLayout === 'quad'
                ? 'bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-400 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                : 'bg-[#12161c] border-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            {viewportLayout === 'quad' ? (
              <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            ) : (
              <Grid2x2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            )}
            <span>{viewportLayout === 'quad' ? 'Quad 4-View Active' : 'Quad 4-View'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Camera Views */}
          {viewportLayout === 'single' && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 p-0.5 rounded-md">
              {(['iso', 'front', 'top', 'right', 'rear'] as ComponentCameraPreset[]).map((cam) => (
                <button
                  key={cam}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCameraPreset({ id: cam, nonce: Date.now() })}
                  className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-[11px] font-medium uppercase transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                    cameraPreset.id === cam
                      ? 'bg-slate-700 text-white font-semibold'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  {cam}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Right Exports & Controls */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          {viewportLayout === 'single' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowCallouts((v) => !v)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                showCallouts
                  ? 'bg-cyan-500/15 border-cyan-500/60 text-cyan-300'
                  : 'bg-[#12161c] border-slate-700 text-slate-400'
              }`}
            >
              <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Leaders &amp; Dims</span>
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setAutoRotate((v) => !v)}
            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition whitespace-nowrap ${
              autoRotate
                ? 'bg-orange-500/15 border-orange-500/60 text-orange-300'
                : 'bg-[#12161c] border-slate-700 text-slate-400'
            }`}
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Orbit Spin</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportSTL}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-700 bg-[#12161c] hover:border-orange-500/60 text-slate-200 hover:text-orange-300 text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <Box className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-400" />
            <span>Export .STL</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportDXF}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-700 bg-[#12161c] hover:border-cyan-500/60 text-slate-200 hover:text-cyan-300 text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <FileCode2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
            <span>Export .DXF</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => captureFnRef.current?.()}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-700 bg-[#12161c] hover:border-emerald-500/60 text-slate-200 hover:text-emerald-300 text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>PNG</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Interactive 3D Viewport + SVG Elbow Leader & Dimension Overlay */}
      <div
        className={`relative w-full h-[520px] overflow-hidden ${
          isIsoPaper ? 'bg-[#f5f6f8]' : 'bg-[#0e1319]'
        }`}
      >
        <div
          ref={mountRef}
          className="w-full h-full cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing"
        />

        {/* Publication Technical Drawing Top Header (visible on ISO Paper mode) */}
        {isIsoPaper && !webglError && (
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-2.5 inset-x-0 text-center z-10 px-4">
            <div className="inline-block px-3 py-0.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold tracking-wide uppercase text-slate-900">
              {entry.partName.split('(')[0].trim()} — {specs.displacementCi} CID, ISOMETRIC TECHNICAL DRAWING
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 60fps Interactive SVG Orthogonal Leader Callouts & ASME Dimension Lines */}
        {viewportLayout === 'single' && showCallouts && !webglError && (
          <svg
            className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 w-full h-full z-10"
            aria-label="Technical Drawing Leader Callouts and Dimensions"
          >
            <defs>
              <marker
                id="dim-arrow-start"
                viewBox="0 0 10 10"
                refX="1"
                refY="5"
                markerWidth="6"
                markerHeight="6"
                orient="auto"
              >
                <path
                  d="M 9 2 L 1 5 L 9 8 z"
                  fill={isIsoPaper ? '#0f172a' : '#38bdf8'}
                />
              </marker>
              <marker
                id="dim-arrow-end"
                viewBox="0 0 10 10"
                refX="9"
                refY="5"
                markerWidth="6"
                markerHeight="6"
                orient="auto"
              >
                <path
                  d="M 1 2 L 9 5 L 1 8 z"
                  fill={isIsoPaper ? '#0f172a' : '#38bdf8'}
                />
              </marker>
            </defs>

            {/* Double-Arrow Extension Dimension Lines */}
            {dimensionBarsMeta.map((dim) => (
              <g
                key={dim.id}
                ref={(el) => {
                  dimGroupRefs.current[dim.id] = el;
                }}
                style={{ opacity: 0 }}
              >
                <line
                  ref={(el) => {
                    dimExt1Refs.current[dim.id] = el;
                  }}
                  stroke={isIsoPaper ? '#475569' : '#64748b'}
                  strokeWidth="0.9"
                  strokeDasharray="3 2"
                />
                <line
                  ref={(el) => {
                    dimExt2Refs.current[dim.id] = el;
                  }}
                  stroke={isIsoPaper ? '#475569' : '#64748b'}
                  strokeWidth="0.9"
                  strokeDasharray="3 2"
                />
                <line
                  ref={(el) => {
                    dimBarRefs.current[dim.id] = el;
                  }}
                  stroke={isIsoPaper ? '#0f172a' : '#38bdf8'}
                  strokeWidth="1.25"
                  markerStart="url(#dim-arrow-start)"
                  markerEnd="url(#dim-arrow-end)"
                />
                <rect
                  ref={(el) => {
                    dimLabelBgRefs.current[dim.id] = el;
                  }}
                  rx="2"
                  fill={isIsoPaper ? 'rgba(245,246,248,0.92)' : 'rgba(13,16,20,0.88)'}
                />
                <text
                  ref={(el) => {
                    dimTextRefs.current[dim.id] = el;
                  }}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  fontFamily="ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace"
                  fontSize="9.5"
                  fontWeight="700"
                  fill={isIsoPaper ? '#0f172a' : '#7dd3fc'}
                >
                  {dim.label}
                </text>
              </g>
            ))}

            {/* Clickable Orthogonal Elbow Leader Callouts */}
            {subPartCatalog.map((sp) => (
              <g
                key={sp.id}
                ref={(el) => {
                  leaderGroupRefs.current[sp.id] = el;
                }}
                style={{ opacity: 0 }}
                className="pointer-events-auto cursor-pointer"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setSelectedSubPartId((prev) => (prev === sp.id ? null : sp.id));
                }}
              >
                <circle
                  ref={(el) => {
                    leaderDotRefs.current[sp.id] = el;
                  }}
                  r="3"
                />
                <path
                  ref={(el) => {
                    leaderPathRefs.current[sp.id] = el;
                  }}
                  fill="none"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                />
                <text
                  ref={(el) => {
                    leaderTextRefs.current[sp.id] = el;
                  }}
                  fontFamily="ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif"
                  fontSize="9.5"
                  letterSpacing="0.03em"
                >
                  {sp.label}
                </text>
              </g>
            ))}
          </svg>
        )}

        {/* Quad-View Quadrant Crosshair & Labels */}
        {viewportLayout === 'quad' && !webglError && (
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-10">
            <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-1/2 w-px bg-cyan-500/40" />
            <div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-px bg-cyan-500/40" />
            <span className="absolute top-2 left-2.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-orange-300 bg-[#0d1014]/90 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700">
              ISO ({projection.toUpperCase()})
            </span>
            <span className="absolute top-2 left-[calc(50%+10px)] font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-cyan-300 bg-[#0d1014]/90 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700">
              TOP PLAN (ORTHO)
            </span>
            <span className="absolute top-[calc(50%+8px)] left-2.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-300 bg-[#0d1014]/90 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700">
              FRONT ELEVATION (ORTHO)
            </span>
            <span className="absolute top-[calc(50%+8px)] left-[calc(50%+10px)] font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 bg-[#0d1014]/90 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700">
              RIGHT PROFILE (ORTHO)
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Title Block HUD Overlay (Bottom Right) */}
        <div
          className={`pointer-events-none absolute bottom-2.5 right-3 z-10 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] space-y-0.5 hidden sm:block border ${
            isIsoPaper
              ? 'bg-white/90 border-slate-300 text-slate-800'
              : 'bg-[#0d1014]/90 border-slate-700/80 text-slate-300'
          }`}
        >
          <div className={`font-bold ${isIsoPaper ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-white'}`}>
            {entry.drawingNumber} · {entry.revision}
          </div>
          <div className={isIsoPaper ? 'text-slate-600' : 'text-slate-400'}>
            {entry.materialStandard} · {entry.surfaceFinishRa.split('|')[0]}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* GD&T Datum Legend when active */}
        {renderMode === 'gdt' && (
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-2.5 left-3 z-10 bg-[#0d1014]/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="text-sky-400 font-semibold">DATUM A (Base)</span>
            <span className="text-orange-400 font-semibold">DATUM B (Rear)</span>
            <span className="text-emerald-400 font-semibold">DATUM C (Side)</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {exportStatus && (
          <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 z-20 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-950/95 border border-emerald-500/60 text-emerald-200 font-mono text-[11px]">
            {exportStatus}
          </div>
        )}

        {webglError && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 z-20 flex items-center justify-center bg-[#0d1014]/95 p-6">
            <div className="max-w-sm text-center space-y-2.5">
              <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white">3D Component Viewport Unavailable</div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{webglError}</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  rendererRef.current?.dispose();
                  rendererRef.current = null;
                  setWebglError(null);
                  setRetryNonce((n) => n + 1);
                }}
                className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition"
              >
                Retry 3D Render
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Individual Sub-Part Quick Selector Rail & Selected Component Inspection Spec Sheet */}
      {subPartCatalog.length > 0 && (
        <div className="px-3 py-2.5 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#10141b] space-y-2.5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1 mr-1">
              <Crosshair className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-400" />
              <span>Select Individual Component:</span>
            </span>
            {subPartCatalog.map((sp) => {
              const active = selectedSubPartId === sp.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={sp.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedSubPartId((prev) => (prev === sp.id ? null : sp.id))}
                  className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-md border font-mono text-[10px] transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                    active
                      ? 'bg-orange-600 border-orange-400 text-white font-bold'
                      : 'bg-[#0d1014] border-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:border-orange-500/60 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  {sp.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {selectedSubPart ? (
            <div className="bg-[#0d1014] border border-orange-500/50 rounded-lg p-3 space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-[10px] text-orange-400 font-bold uppercase">
                    SELECTED COMPONENT · {selectedSubPart.category}
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                    {selectedSubPart.label}
                  </h4>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedSubPartId(null)}
                  className="p-1 rounded border border-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:border-slate-500 transition"
                  title="Clear component selection"
                >
                  <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2 text-[11px]">
                <div className="bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase">Material Spec</div>
                  <div className="text-slate-100 font-medium mt-0.5">{selectedSubPart.material}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase">Parametric Dimensions</div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-cyan-300 font-semibold tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                    {selectedSubPart.dimensions}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase">GD&amp;T Tolerance</div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-amber-300 font-semibold tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                    {selectedSubPart.tolerance}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase">Flow / Torque Rating</div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-emerald-300 font-semibold tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                    {selectedSubPart.flowOrTorque}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 leading-relaxed border-t border-slate-800 pt-1.5">
                {selectedSubPart.notes}
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              Click any 3D sub-part mesh, any leader callout label, or any component button above to inspect its material, tolerance band, and live modification geometry.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Bottom Live Cross-Section Plane Slicer Bar */}
      <div className="px-3 py-2 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#0d1014] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Scissors className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
          <span className="text-slate-300 font-medium">Section Cut Plane:</span>
          {(['none', 'x', 'y', 'z'] as SectionAxis[]).map((ax) => (
            <button
              key={ax}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSectionAxis(ax)}
              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-mono uppercase transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                sectionAxis === ax
                  ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 font-bold'
                  : 'bg-[#12161c] border border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {ax === 'none' ? 'Off' : `${ax}-Axis`}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {sectionAxis !== 'none' ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 max-w-sm">
            <input
              type="range"
              min="-1"
              max="1"
              step="0.02"
              value={sectionPercent}
              onChange={(e) => setSectionPercent(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full accent-cyan-400 cursor-pointer h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded"
            />
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-cyan-300 tabular-nums w-14 text-right">
              {Math.round(sectionPercent * 100)}%
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            Drag to orbit · Scroll to zoom · Select X/Y/Z axis to inspect internal walls &amp; oil galleries
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

function detectSoftwareRenderer(gl: WebGLRenderingContext | WebGL2RenderingContext | null): boolean {
  if (!gl) return true;
  try {
    const ext = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
    const param = ext ? ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL : gl.RENDERER;
    const gpu = String(gl.getParameter(param) || '');
    return /SwiftShader|llvmpipe|softpipe|Software|Basic Render/i.test(gpu);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

  selectedPart,
  measurementState,
  onUpdateMeasurement,
  techDrawing = null,
  focusPartRequest = null,
  onOpenDrawingsTab,
  onTelemetryUpdate,
  onHoverProbe
}) => {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const rendererRef = useRef<THREE.WebGLRenderer | null>(null);
  const [webglError, setWebglError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [retryNonce, setRetryNonce] = useState<number>(0);
  const isRunningRef = useRef(isRunning);
  const rpmRef = useRef(rpm);
  const explodedRef = useRef(explodedAmount);
  const sectionOffsetRef = useRef(sectionOffset);
  const hiddenPartsRef = useRef(hiddenParts);
  const measurementRef = useRef(measurementState);
  const selectedPartRef = useRef(selectedPart);
  const onSelectPartRef = useRef(onSelectPart);
  const onUpdateMeasurementRef = useRef(onUpdateMeasurement);
  const onRegisterCaptureRef = useRef(onRegisterCapture);
  const onRegisterStlExportRef = useRef(onRegisterStlExport);
  const onTelemetryUpdateRef = useRef(onTelemetryUpdate);
  const onHoverProbeRef = useRef(onHoverProbe);
  const specsRef = useRef(specs);
  const configRef = useRef(config);
  const enginePartsRef = useRef<Record<string, THREE.Object3D>>({});
  const engineReticleStateRef = useRef<{
    reticle: THREE.Mesh;
    camera: THREE.Camera;
  } | null>(null);
  const camTransitionRef = useRef<{ active: boolean; pos: THREE.Vector3 }>({
    active: false,
    pos: new THREE.Vector3(...CAMERA_PRESET_COORDS.iso)
  });

  useEffect(() => { isRunningRef.current = isRunning; }, [isRunning]);
  useEffect(() => { rpmRef.current = rpm; }, [rpm]);
  useEffect(() => { explodedRef.current = explodedAmount; }, [explodedAmount]);
  useEffect(() => { sectionOffsetRef.current = sectionOffset; }, [sectionOffset]);
  useEffect(() => { hiddenPartsRef.current = hiddenParts; }, [hiddenParts]);
  useEffect(() => { measurementRef.current = measurementState; }, [measurementState]);
  useEffect(() => { selectedPartRef.current = selectedPart; }, [selectedPart]);
  useEffect(() => { onSelectPartRef.current = onSelectPart; }, [onSelectPart]);
  useEffect(() => { onUpdateMeasurementRef.current = onUpdateMeasurement; }, [onUpdateMeasurement]);
  useEffect(() => { onRegisterCaptureRef.current = onRegisterCapture; }, [onRegisterCapture]);
  useEffect(() => { onRegisterStlExportRef.current = onRegisterStlExport; }, [onRegisterStlExport]);
  useEffect(() => { onTelemetryUpdateRef.current = onTelemetryUpdate; }, [onTelemetryUpdate]);
  useEffect(() => { onHoverProbeRef.current = onHoverProbe; }, [onHoverProbe]);
  useEffect(() => { specsRef.current = specs; }, [specs]);
  useEffect(() => { configRef.current = config; }, [config]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!cameraPreset) return;
    const coords = CAMERA_PRESET_COORDS[cameraPreset.id];
    camTransitionRef.current.pos.set(coords[0], coords[1], coords[2]);
    camTransitionRef.current.active = true;
  }, [cameraPreset]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (rendererRef.current) {
        rendererRef.current.dispose();
        rendererRef.current = null;
      }
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const container = mountRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    const width = container.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    const height = container.clientHeight || 560;

    const isDrawing = viewMode === 'drawing';
    const isIsoPaper = viewMode === 'iso_paper';
    const isWire = viewMode === 'wireframe';
    const isGhost = viewMode === 'ghost';
    const isStudio = viewMode === 'realistic';
    const isCinematic = viewMode === 'cinematic';

    let candidateRenderer = rendererRef.current;
    if (!candidateRenderer) {
      try {
        candidateRenderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
          antialias: true,
          powerPreference: 'default',
          preserveDrawingBuffer: true
        });
        rendererRef.current = candidateRenderer;
      } catch (err) {
        setWebglError(
          err instanceof Error
            ? err.message
            : 'WebGL 3D context could not be initialized by your browser.'
        );
        return;
      }
    }
    const renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer = candidateRenderer;
    const initialGl = renderer.getContext();
    if (!initialGl) {
      setWebglError('WebGL rendering context is unavailable or was lost.');
      return;
    }
    setWebglError(null);

    const isSoftwareGl = detectSoftwareRenderer(initialGl);

    // 2. Scene, Fog & Renderer Setup
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const cinematicBg =
      cinematicPreset === 'showroom'
        ? 0x05070b
        : cinematicPreset === 'machine_shop'
        ? 0x0a0d12
        : 0x06080d;
    const bgHex = isCinematic
      ? cinematicBg
      : isIsoPaper
      ? 0xf5f6f8
      : isDrawing
      ? 0x091628
      : isWire
      ? 0x07111a
      : isStudio
      ? 0xdce1e7
      : 0x101419;
    scene.background = new THREE.Color(bgHex);
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(
      bgHex,
      isCinematic ? 0.013 : isIsoPaper ? 0.0022 : isStudio ? 0.004 : 0.011
    );

    renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = isCinematic ? 1.18 : isIsoPaper ? 1.14 : isStudio ? 1.08 : 1.16;
    renderer.localClippingEnabled = true;
    renderer.setSize(width, height);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(isSoftwareGl ? 1 : Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.shadowMap.enabled = !isSoftwareGl;
    renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;

    if (renderer.domElement.parentNode !== container) {
      container.innerHTML = '';
      container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
    }

    // 3. Camera & OrbitControls (Perspective or True CAD Orthographic)
    const aspect = width / height;
    const orthoHalf = 9.5;
    const camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera | THREE.OrthographicCamera =
      projectionMode === 'orthographic'
        ? new THREE.OrthographicCamera(
            -orthoHalf * aspect,
            orthoHalf * aspect,
            orthoHalf,
            -orthoHalf,
            -120,
            400
          )
        : new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(isCinematic ? 36 : 40, aspect, 0.1, 400);
    camera.position.set(17.5, 11.5, 20.5);
    camera.lookAt(0, 1.0, 0);

    const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
    controls.enableDamping = !isSoftwareGl;
    controls.dampingFactor = 0.06;
    controls.minDistance = 6.5;
    controls.maxDistance = 48;
    controls.target.set(0, 1.0, 0);
    controls.addEventListener('start', () => {
      camTransitionRef.current.active = false;
    });

    // 4. Studio Environment Map (RoomEnvironment) + 3-Point Warm/Cool Lighting + Softboxes
    const pmremGenerator = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
    scene.environment = pmremGenerator.fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture;
    pmremGenerator.dispose();

    const hemiLight = new THREE.HemisphereLight(
      isCinematic
        ? cinematicPreset === 'machine_shop'
          ? 0xfef3c7
          : 0xbae6fd
        : isIsoPaper || isStudio
        ? 0xffffff
        : 0xcbe4ff,
      isCinematic ? 0x1e293b : isIsoPaper ? 0x94a3b8 : isStudio ? 0x64748b : 0x2a1f18,
      isCinematic ? 0.85 : isIsoPaper ? 1.25 : isStudio ? 1.05 : 0.72
    );
    scene.add(hemiLight);

    const keyColor =
      isCinematic && cinematicPreset === 'machine_shop'
        ? 0xffedd5
        : isCinematic && cinematicPreset === 'showroom'
        ? 0xf8fafc
        : 0xfff7ed;
    const keyLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(
      keyColor,
      isCinematic ? 2.25 : isIsoPaper ? 1.95 : isStudio ? 1.85 : 1.65
    );
    keyLight.position.set(18, 25, 18);
    if (!isSoftwareGl) {
      keyLight.castShadow = true;
      keyLight.shadow.mapSize.width = isCinematic ? 2048 : 1024;
      keyLight.shadow.mapSize.height = isCinematic ? 2048 : 1024;
      keyLight.shadow.camera.near = 2;
      keyLight.shadow.camera.far = 65;
      keyLight.shadow.camera.left = -14;
      keyLight.shadow.camera.right = 14;
      keyLight.shadow.camera.top = 14;
      keyLight.shadow.camera.bottom = -14;
      keyLight.shadow.bias = -0.0005;
      keyLight.shadow.normalBias = 0.02;
    }
    scene.add(keyLight);

    const rimLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(
      isCinematic
        ? cinematicPreset === 'machine_shop'
          ? 0x38bdf8
          : 0x67e8f9
        : isIsoPaper || isStudio
        ? 0xe0f2fe
        : 0x7cc4ff,
      isCinematic ? 1.45 : isIsoPaper || isStudio ? 0.95 : 0.9
    );
    rimLight.position.set(-18, 14, -18);
    scene.add(rimLight);

    const underLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(
      isCinematic ? 0xfb923c : 0xffcf99,
      isCinematic ? 0.65 : isStudio ? 0.55 : 0.45
    );
    underLight.position.set(2, -12, 8);
    scene.add(underLight);

    if (!isSoftwareGl) {
      RectAreaLightUniformsLib.init();
      const softboxTop = new THREE.RectAreaLight(
        0xffffff,
        isCinematic ? 4.4 : isIsoPaper || isStudio ? 3.2 : 2.4,
        16,
        7
      );
      softboxTop.position.set(0, 14, 4);
      softboxTop.lookAt(0, 1, 0);
      scene.add(softboxTop);

      if (isCinematic) {
        const softboxSide = new THREE.RectAreaLight(0x38bdf8, 2.8, 12, 5);
        softboxSide.position.set(-14, 4, -8);
        softboxSide.lookAt(0, 0.5, 0);
        scene.add(softboxSide);
      }
    }

    // 5. Procedural CAD Textures & Stage Grounding
    const maxAniso = isSoftwareGl ? 1 : Math.min(8, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy());
    const textures = createProceduralTextureSet(maxAniso);

    const stagePlane = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(34, 34),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        map: isIsoPaper || isStudio ? textures.studioFloorShadowMap : textures.blueprintScaleMap,
        color: isCinematic ? 0x0b1017 : 0xffffff,
        roughness: isCinematic ? 0.28 : 0.9,
        metalness: isCinematic ? 0.35 : 0.0,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: isCinematic ? 0.92 : isIsoPaper ? 0.42 : isStudio ? 0.92 : 0.85,
        depthWrite: isCinematic
      })
    );
    stagePlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    stagePlane.position.y = -5.78;
    stagePlane.receiveShadow = !isSoftwareGl;
    scene.add(stagePlane);

    const grid = new THREE.GridHelper(
      34,
      34,
      isCinematic ? 0x1e293b : isIsoPaper ? 0xcbd5e1 : isDrawing ? 0x38bdf8 : isStudio ? 0x94a3b8 : 0x0ea5e9,
      isCinematic ? 0x0f172a : isIsoPaper ? 0xe2e8f0 : isDrawing ? 0x16324f : isStudio ? 0xc5cdd8 : 0x1c2833
    );
    grid.position.y = -5.80;
    scene.add(grid);

    // 6. Cutaway Clipping Planes & Isolation Helpers
    let activeClippingPlanes: THREE.Plane[] = [];
    let baseClipConstant = 0;
    if (cutawayMode === 'rightBank') {
      // Diagonal front-right museum cutaway: preserves rear half & left bank while slicing open
      // front-right valve cover, cylinder head, block bores, and timing cover.
      baseClipConstant = 2.15;
      activeClippingPlanes = [
        new THREE.Plane(new THREE.Vector3(-0.68, -0.38, -0.62).normalize(), baseClipConstant)
      ];
    } else if (cutawayMode === 'centerLongitudinal') {
      baseClipConstant = 0.15;
      activeClippingPlanes = [new THREE.Plane(new THREE.Vector3(-1, 0, 0), baseClipConstant)];
    } else if (cutawayMode === 'valvetrain') {
      baseClipConstant = 3.75;
      activeClippingPlanes = [new THREE.Plane(new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0), baseClipConstant)];
    }

    const isIsolated = (sub: SubsystemIsolation) =>
      subsystemIsolation === 'all' || subsystemIsolation === sub;
    const getAlpha = (sub: SubsystemIsolation) => (isIsolated(sub) ? 1.0 : 0.11);

    // 7. Performance PBR Materials Library (with SIMULIA FEA Stress / Thermal / Clearance mapping)
    const isStress = analysisMode === 'stress';
    const isThermal = analysisMode === 'thermal';
    const isClearance = analysisMode === 'clearance';
    const quenchPass = specs.quenchDistance >= 0.038 && specs.quenchDistance <= 0.062;

    const mats = {
      chevyBlock: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress
          ? 0x1d4ed8
          : isThermal
          ? 0x0284c7
          : isClearance
          ? 0x334155
          : isDrawing
          ? 0x162a45
          : isIsoPaper
          ? 0x94a3b8
          : 0xd83b01,
        roughness: isDrawing ? 0.85 : isIsoPaper ? 0.48 : 0.72,
        metalness: isDrawing ? 0.1 : isIsoPaper ? 0.48 : 0.24,
        bumpMap: isDrawing || isIsoPaper || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.castIronBump,
        bumpScale: 0.032,
        normalMap: isDrawing || isIsoPaper || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.castIronNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.72, 0.72),
        clearcoat: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? 0 : 0.08,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('all'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.30 : getAlpha('all'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      machinedDeck: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0x06b6d4 : isThermal ? 0x38bdf8 : isClearance ? 0x22d3ee : isDrawing ? 0x1f3b5c : 0xdde3ea,
        map: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedSwirl,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.42, 0.42),
        roughness: 0.20,
        metalness: 0.90,
        anisotropy: 0.65,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('all') && !isIsolated('rotating'),
        opacity: isIsolated('all') ? 1.0 : Math.max(getAlpha('all'), getAlpha('rotating')),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      machinedInternal: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0x06b6d4 : isThermal ? 0x38bdf8 : isClearance ? 0x22d3ee : isDrawing ? 0x1f3b5c : 0xdde3ea,
        map: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedSwirl,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.40, 0.40),
        roughness: 0.18,
        metalness: 0.92,
        anisotropy: 0.68,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('rotating') && !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: isIsolated('all') ? 1.0 : Math.max(getAlpha('rotating'), getAlpha('valvetrain'))
      }),
      sleeveBore: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0x22c55e : isThermal ? 0xf59e0b : isClearance ? (quenchPass ? 0x10b981 : 0xef4444) : 0x94a3b8,
        map: analysisMode === 'none' ? textures.crosshatchHone : null,
        roughness: 0.18,
        metalness: 0.88,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('all'),
        opacity: getAlpha('all'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      crankForged: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0xf97316 : isThermal ? 0x22c55e : isClearance ? 0x38bdf8 : isDrawing ? 0x2b4f75 : 0xe2e8f0,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.28, 0.28),
        roughness: 0.13,
        metalness: 0.96,
        anisotropy: 0.78,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('rotating'),
        opacity: getAlpha('rotating')
      }),
      rods4340: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0xef4444 : isThermal ? 0x10b981 : isClearance ? (specs.rodStrokeRatio >= 1.6 ? 0x10b981 : 0xf59e0b) : isDrawing ? 0x213d5c : 0x64748b,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.castIronNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.32, 0.32),
        roughness: 0.30,
        metalness: 0.88,
        anisotropy: 0.55,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('rotating'),
        opacity: getAlpha('rotating')
      }),
      pistonsAlloy: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0xfacc15 : isThermal ? 0xf97316 : isClearance ? (quenchPass ? 0x10b981 : 0xef4444) : isDrawing ? 0x335e8c : 0xe2e8f0,
        map: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.pistonSkirtMoly,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.35, 0.35),
        roughness: 0.17,
        metalness: 0.86,
        anisotropy: 0.65,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('rotating'),
        opacity: getAlpha('rotating')
      }),
      wristPins: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0xef4444 : isThermal ? 0xf59e0b : 0xf8fafc,
        roughness: 0.08,
        metalness: 0.98,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('rotating'),
        opacity: getAlpha('rotating')
      }),
      arpBlackOxide: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0xeab308 : 0x1e293b,
        bumpMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.boltThreadBump,
        bumpScale: 0.014,
        roughness: 0.24,
        metalness: 0.92,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      brassBronze: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xd97706,
        roughness: 0.28,
        metalness: 0.88,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      felProCopper: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isClearance ? (quenchPass ? 0x22c55e : 0xef4444) : isThermal ? 0xfacc15 : 0xc26d38,
        roughness: 0.26,
        metalness: 0.85,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('all'),
        opacity: getAlpha('all')
      }),
      aluminumHeads: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0x3b82f6 : isThermal ? 0xfb923c : isClearance ? 0x475569 : isDrawing ? 0x1b3452 : 0xc4cbd2,
        bumpMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.castIronBump,
        bumpScale: 0.015,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.castIronNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.50, 0.50),
        roughness: 0.34,
        metalness: 0.72,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.28 : getAlpha('valvetrain'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      billetRockersBlue: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0x22c55e : 0x0284c7,
        roughness: 0.22,
        metalness: 0.88,
        anisotropy: 0.6,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: getAlpha('valvetrain')
      }),
      valveSpringsGold: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0xf97316 : 0xeab308,
        roughness: 0.26,
        metalness: 0.88,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: getAlpha('valvetrain')
      }),
      valvesSteel: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isThermal ? 0xef4444 : isClearance ? 0x22d3ee : 0xf1f5f9,
        roughness: 0.14,
        metalness: 0.94,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: getAlpha('valvetrain')
      }),
      chromePolish: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x3b6998 : 0xf8fafc,
        roughness: 0.08,
        metalness: 0.98,
        clearcoat: 0.6,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('all'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.32 : getAlpha('all'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      standChrome: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x3b6998 : 0xe2e8f0,
        roughness: 0.14,
        metalness: 0.92,
        clearcoat: 0.4,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost,
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.35 : 1.0
      }),
      valveCoverBody: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isIsoPaper ? 0xe2e8f0 : isDrawing ? 0x1f3b5c : 0x1e242c,
        roughness: isIsoPaper ? 0.14 : 0.38,
        metalness: isIsoPaper ? 0.12 : 0.55,
        clearcoat: isIsoPaper ? 0.85 : 0.3,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isIsoPaper || isGhost || !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: isIsoPaper ? 0.36 : isGhost ? 0.26 : getAlpha('valvetrain'),
        depthWrite: !isIsoPaper,
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      valveCoverFin: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x3b6998 : 0xdbe2e9,
        roughness: 0.16,
        metalness: 0.92,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isIsoPaper || isGhost || !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: isIsoPaper ? 0.55 : isGhost ? 0.28 : getAlpha('valvetrain'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      headerCeramic: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isThermal ? 0xef4444 : isDrawing ? 0x2b4f75 : 0x9ca3af,
        roughness: 0.25,
        metalness: 0.85,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('all'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.25 : getAlpha('all'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      damperTiming: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        map: textures.harmonicTimingTape,
        roughness: 0.35,
        metalness: 0.45,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('rotating'),
        opacity: getAlpha('rotating')
      }),
      ignitionRed: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xd92626,
        roughness: 0.35,
        metalness: 0.25,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('ignition'),
        opacity: getAlpha('ignition')
      }),
      sparkPlugWhite: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xffffff,
        roughness: 0.12,
        metalness: 0.08,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('ignition'),
        opacity: getAlpha('ignition')
      }),
      oilPumpBronze: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xb45309,
        roughness: 0.34,
        metalness: 0.82,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('lubrication'),
        opacity: getAlpha('lubrication')
      }),
      filterBlue: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x1d4ed8,
        roughness: 0.28,
        metalness: 0.5,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('lubrication'),
        opacity: getAlpha('lubrication')
      }),
      coolingAlum: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x94a3b8,
        roughness: 0.38,
        metalness: 0.65,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('cooling'),
        opacity: getAlpha('cooling')
      }),
      beltsBlack: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x14171a,
        roughness: 0.85,
        metalness: 0.05,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('cooling'),
        opacity: getAlpha('cooling')
      }),
      fuelBlueAnodized: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x0284c7,
        roughness: 0.2,
        metalness: 0.88,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('fuel'),
        opacity: getAlpha('fuel')
      }),
      starterDark: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x1e293b,
        roughness: 0.35,
        metalness: 0.82,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('starter'),
        opacity: getAlpha('starter')
      }),
      techEdgeLine: new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
        color: isIsoPaper ? 0x1e293b : 0x38bdf8,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: isIsoPaper ? 0.72 : isDrawing ? 0.92 : 0.45
      }),
      billetAlum: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isStress ? 0x3b82f6 : isThermal ? 0xfb923c : isDrawing ? 0x24466b : 0xdbe2e9,
        map: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedSwirl,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.38, 0.38),
        roughness: 0.20,
        metalness: 0.88,
        anisotropy: 0.6,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('valvetrain'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.30 : getAlpha('valvetrain'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      carbGoldZinc: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x3b6998 : 0xc9a24b,
        roughness: 0.28,
        metalness: 0.82,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('fuel'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.32 : isIsolated('all') ? 1.0 : getAlpha('fuel')
      }),
      copperWinding: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xc26d27,
        roughness: 0.26,
        metalness: 0.88,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      braidedStainless: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        map: textures.braidedSteelMap,
        color: 0xe2e8f0,
        roughness: 0.36,
        metalness: 0.72,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      coilBlue: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: 0x1d4ed8,
        roughness: 0.26,
        metalness: 0.58,
        clearcoat: 0.45,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: !isIsolated('ignition'),
        opacity: getAlpha('ignition')
      }),
      chassisBlack: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x1b3452 : 0x1a1e24,
        roughness: 0.48,
        metalness: 0.48,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost,
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.35 : 1.0
      }),
      anodizedBlack: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x181c22,
        roughness: 0.30,
        metalness: 0.76,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      anRed: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0xdc2626,
        roughness: 0.24,
        metalness: 0.84,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      radiatorFin: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        map: textures.radiatorCoreMap,
        color: 0xd8dee6,
        roughness: 0.38,
        metalness: 0.76,
        wireframe: isWire
      }),
      oilPanCoat: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x162a45 : 0xd83b01,
        roughness: 0.46,
        metalness: 0.35,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('lubrication'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.28 : isIsolated('all') ? 1.0 : getAlpha('lubrication'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      }),
      timingCoverAlum: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: isDrawing ? 0x1f3b5c : 0xd2d9e1,
        map: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedSwirl,
        normalMap: isDrawing || analysisMode !== 'none' ? null : textures.machinedNormal,
        normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.42, 0.42),
        roughness: 0.24,
        metalness: 0.86,
        anisotropy: 0.5,
        wireframe: isWire,
        transparent: isGhost || !isIsolated('all'),
        opacity: isGhost ? 0.28 : getAlpha('all'),
        clippingPlanes: activeClippingPlanes
      })
    };

    const engineGroup = new THREE.Group();
    const parts: Record<string, THREE.Object3D> = {};

    // Real-World Scaled Kinematic Constants (1 scene unit ≈ 1.48 inches on engine centerline)
    const crankCenterY = -1.0;
    const boreRadius = (config.bore / 4.000) * 1.02;
    const crankRadius = (config.stroke / 3.480) * 0.64;
    const scaledRodLen = crankRadius * (config.rodLength / (config.stroke * 0.5));
    const journalZ = [-3.9, -1.3, 1.3, 3.9];
    const crankPhases = [0, Math.PI / 2, (3 * Math.PI) / 2, Math.PI];

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. CAST-IRON 90° V8 BLOCK CASTING, 4-BOLT SPLAYED MAIN CAPS & BORES
    // =========================================================================
    const blockGroup = new THREE.Group();

    const blockCasting = new THREE.Mesh(createV8BlockProfileGeometry(11.4), mats.chevyBlock);
    blockCasting.position.y = -0.2;
    blockCasting.castShadow = true;
    blockCasting.receiveShadow = true;
    blockCasting.userData = {
      name: 'SBC 350 Cast-Iron 4-Bolt Main Block (GM 10243880)',
      category: 'Engine Block',
      desc: `90° V8 sand-cast iron block. Deck height: ${config.deckHeight.toFixed(3)}" | Bore: ${config.bore.toFixed(3)}" | Quench: ${specs.quenchDistance}".`,
      torque: 'Main Caps: 70 ft-lbs (inner) / 65 ft-lbs (splayed outer)',
      partNumber: 'GM-10243880'
    };
    blockGroup.add(blockCasting);

    // 5 Splayed 4-Bolt Main Bearing Caps
    for (let m = 0; m < 5; m++) {
      const capZ = -5.2 + m * 2.6;
      const capBridge = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.4, 1.15, 0.85), mats.machinedDeck);
      capBridge.position.set(0, -1.85, capZ);
      blockGroup.add(capBridge);

      // Inner vertical & splayed outer ARP main bolts
      [-0.85, 0.85].forEach((xOff) => {
        const innerBolt = createARP12PointBolt(0.11, 1.2, mats.arpBlackOxide);
        innerBolt.position.set(xOff, -2.42, capZ);
        innerBolt.rotation.x = Math.PI;
        blockGroup.add(innerBolt);
      });
      [-1.42, 1.42].forEach((xOff, i) => {
        const splayedBolt = createARP12PointBolt(0.10, 1.25, mats.arpBlackOxide);
        splayedBolt.position.set(xOff, -2.35, capZ);
        splayedBolt.rotation.z = i === 0 ? -0.28 : 0.28;
        splayedBolt.rotation.x = Math.PI;
        blockGroup.add(splayedBolt);
      });
    }

    // 8 Plateau-Honed Cylinder Bore Sleeves & Brass Freeze Plugs
    [-1, 1].forEach((side) => {
      // 3 Brass Core Freeze Plugs along outer block skirt
      [-3.5, 0, 3.5].forEach((zPlug) => {
        const freezePlug = new THREE.Mesh(
          new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.46, 0.44, 0.16, 20),
          mats.brassBronze
        );
        freezePlug.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
        freezePlug.position.set(side * 3.35, -0.45, zPlug);
        freezePlug.userData = {
          name: '1-5/8" Brass Core Freeze Plug',
          category: 'Engine Block',
          desc: 'Deep-cup brass expansion core plug sealed with Permatex #2.'
        };
        blockGroup.add(freezePlug);
      });

      // Honed Cylinder Sleeves + Deck Fire Ring
      for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
        const zOff = journalZ[c] + (side === 1 ? 0.22 : -0.22);
        const bankRot = -side * (Math.PI / 4);
        const sleeve = new THREE.Mesh(
          new THREE.CylinderGeometry(boreRadius + 0.04, boreRadius + 0.04, 2.75, 28, 1, true),
          mats.sleeveBore
        );
        const midDist = 2.05;
        sleeve.position.set(
          -Math.sin(bankRot) * midDist,
          crankCenterY + Math.cos(bankRot) * midDist,
          zOff
        );
        sleeve.rotation.z = bankRot;
        blockGroup.add(sleeve);
      }

      // Machined Cylinder Deck Plane + Fel-Pro 1003 (.039") Copper/Composite Head Gasket
      const bankRot = -side * (Math.PI / 4);
      const deckDist = 3.22;
      const deckPlate = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.2, 0.14, 11.4),
        mats.machinedDeck
      );
      deckPlate.position.set(
        -Math.sin(bankRot) * deckDist,
        crankCenterY + Math.cos(bankRot) * deckDist,
        0
      );
      deckPlate.rotation.z = bankRot;
      blockGroup.add(deckPlate);

      const headGasket = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.15, 0.07, 11.35),
        mats.felProCopper
      );
      headGasket.position.set(
        -Math.sin(bankRot) * (deckDist + 0.11),
        crankCenterY + Math.cos(bankRot) * (deckDist + 0.11),
        0
      );
      headGasket.rotation.z = bankRot;
      headGasket.userData = {
        name: `Fel-Pro 1003 Head Gasket (${config.gasketThickness.toFixed(3)}" Compressed)`,
        category: 'Sealing & Quench',
        desc: `Pre-flattened copper wire ring armor. Contributes ${specs.gasketVolumeCc}cc to clearance volume; sets total quench to ${specs.quenchDistance}".`,
        partNumber: 'FEL-1003'
      };
      blockGroup.add(headGasket);

      // 3-Bolt Motor Mount Casting Boss on SBC Block Side
      const mountBoss = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.45, 0.82, 1.35),
        mats.chevyBlock
      );
      mountBoss.position.set(side * 3.15, -1.35, 0.55);
      blockGroup.add(mountBoss);

      // Hollow Deck Alignment Dowel Pins (front & rear deck corners)
      [-4.95, 4.95].forEach((dZ) => {
        const dowel = new THREE.Mesh(
          new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14, 0.14, 0.24, 14, 1, true),
          mats.machinedInternal
        );
        dowel.position.set(
          -Math.sin(bankRot) * (deckDist + 0.08) + Math.cos(bankRot) * (side * 1.15),
          crankCenterY + Math.cos(bankRot) * (deckDist + 0.08) + Math.sin(bankRot) * (side * 1.15),
          dZ
        );
        dowel.rotation.z = bankRot;
        blockGroup.add(dowel);
      });
    });

    // Dura-Bond Cam Tunnel Babbit Bearings + Front/Rear 1/4" NPT Brass Oil Gallery Plugs
    for (let m = 0; m < 5; m++) {
      const camBearing = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.45, 0.45, 0.46, 20, 1, true),
        mats.brassBronze
      );
      camBearing.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
      camBearing.position.set(0, 1.05, -5.2 + m * 2.6);
      blockGroup.add(camBearing);
    }
